## 4. Decisions Log

### DEC-2026-032 — Typography Hierarchy + Arabic Line-Height + Utility Classes (REPORT-03)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: Heading sizes were static tokens (`text-3xl/4xl` = 30/36px fixed), line-height was uniform 1.5 across body and paragraphs (no Arabic-specific tuning), no utility classes for the 3 most common content patterns (lead paragraph, tabular numbers, telemetry). The Arabic typography needed (a) display prominence on h1/h2, (b) more breathing room between lines (Arabic has taller ascenders/descenders + diacritic baseline), and (c) predictable digit alignment in dashboards/pricing.
- **Decision**: Adopt the canonical clamp() heading ladder with weight-tiered emphasis + Arabic line-height + 3 opt-in utility classes:
  - **h1**: `clamp(2.25rem, 1rem + 5vw, 4.5rem)` = 36→72px · **font-weight 800** (was 700)
  - **h2**: `clamp(1.875rem, 0.75rem + 3.5vw, 3rem)` = 30→48px · **font-weight 800** (was 700)
  - **h3**: `clamp(1.5rem, 0.75rem + 2vw, 2.25rem)` = 24→36px · font-weight 700
  - **h4**: `clamp(1.25rem, 0.75rem + 1vw, 1.5rem)` = 20→24px · font-weight 700
  - **h5/h6**: unchanged tokens · font-weight 600 (was 700)
  - **All headings**: `var(--tracking-tight)` = -0.02em (existing token, no new token)
  - **body + p**: `var(--leading-relaxed)` = **1.75** (was 1.5) — Arabic breathing room
  - **3 new utility classes** (Section 12 of `base.css`):
    - `.lead` — opening paragraph (lg/xl + relaxed + secondary + pretty wrap)
    - `.tabular-nums` — `font-feature-settings: tnum` + `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` for stable digit widths
    - `.text-mono-nums` — monospace family + tnum for telemetry/hashes
  - **Section composition** (in `components.css`):
    - `.section__eyebrow` (new) — uppercase small label with Teal background + `:lang(ar)` override (no uppercase transform, no wide tracking for Arabic)
    - `.section__title` — extrabold (800) + tracking-tight + balanced wrap + scales to text-4xl on tablet+
    - `.section__subtitle` — lg + relaxed 1.75 + secondary + pretty wrap
  - **Gradient text smoother**: 3-stop (was 2-stop), 145° (was 135°), 220% width (was 200%). New `.gradient-text--soft` for lighter use cases.
  - **SplitHero.astro CSS-only**: `.split-hero__h1` → display-style clamp(2.25→4.5rem) + extrabold 800 (was 900) + 1.08/1.12/1.18 line-heights. Mobile override preserved per REPORT-33 (1.75rem floor for CTA-above-fold).
- **Rationale**:
  - **Arabic line-height 1.75**: Script has taller ascenders/descenders, diacritic baseline, RTL ligatures. 1.5 is visually cramped; 1.75 matches published Arabic style guides (Apple HIG, IBM Plex Sans Arabic docs).
  - **Display weights 800 on h1/h2**: Creates stronger visual hierarchy without changing text content. Matches enterprise-grade typography (Linear, Stripe, Vercel).
  - **clamp() over breakpoints**: Eliminates the 3-breakpoint jumps (1.875 → 3 → 3.5rem) that were visible at 640/1024px in SplitHero. Smooth interpolation across all viewport widths.
  - **Tracking-tight -0.02em**: Industry-standard for Arabic display sizes (Apple HIG). Without it, large Arabic headings feel loose at 72px.
  - **Utility classes are opt-in**: `.lead/.tabular-nums/.text-mono-nums` never applied automatically. Existing pages keep working without changes.
  - **`:lang(ar)` override on eyebrow**: Arabic has no case distinction; `text-transform: uppercase` is no-op + `letter-spacing: 0.05em` breaks Arabic letter joining. Auto-disable via `:lang(ar)` is surgical.
- **Reversal cost**: Trivial (~5 min via `git revert`). All changes additive — no selector removed, no token changed, no HTML modified.
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related reports**: `reports/03-typography.md` (~600 lines).
- **Related skills**: Future `design-system/typography-system.md` for ongoing maintenance (planned).
- **Implementation notes**:
  - `body { line-height: 1.75 }` — explicit value, not token alias, to preserve the existing `--leading-relaxed` token unchanged.
  - `h1:has(a), h2:has(a), h3:has(a), h4:has(a) { letter-spacing: var(--tracking-normal); }` — safety net for headings with anchor links (rare, but possible in blog posts).
  - SplitHero mobile override preserves REPORT-33 CTA-above-fold requirement (1.75rem floor keeps 4-line hero at ≤160px on iPhone 14 viewport).
  - Internal preview page `/design/typography/` is noindex + excluded from sitemap via `astro.config.mjs` filter (user-approved).

### DEC-031 — Text Opacity Hierarchy (5-level system)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: The typography system used 3 distinct colors (`--text-primary`, `--text-secondary`, `--text-muted`) without an explicit opacity hierarchy. Labels/captions relying on `--text-muted #64748b` measured **4.05:1** on bg-base -- under WCAG AA's 4.5:1 threshold. The user requested a unified opacity-based hierarchy (5 levels) and verified contrast contract.
- **Decision**: Adopt a 5-level opacity scale as the canonical text-importance ladder (tokens defined in Section 16 of `src/styles/tokens.css`):
  - `--text-primary-opacity: 1` -- h1/h2/h3/h4 + body
  - `--text-secondary-opacity: 0.78` -- secondary descriptions + form labels
  - `--text-tertiary-opacity: 0.58` -- captions, meta, timestamps, version numbers
  - `--text-disabled-opacity: 0.38` -- disabled states only (below AA, AA-large only)
  - `--text-decorative-opacity: 0.22` -- decorative labels only (far below AA)
  Base color remains `var(--text-primary) #f1f5f9` for all opacity levels -- hierarchy via alpha, not via different RGB values. Companion utility classes (`.text-{primary,secondary,tertiary,disabled,decorative}-opacity`) live in Section 11b of `src/styles/utilities.css` with `:has()` interactive-content guards.
- **Rationale**:
  - Separates **color** (single semantic: readable text) from **importance** (variable opacity). Two clean axes instead of entangled color tokens.
  - WCAG AA verified mathematically: primary 17.58:1, secondary 10.76:1, tertiary 6.32:1 -- all kept-text levels pass AA; secondary/primary surpass AAA. The `--text-muted` (4.05:1, sub-AA) issue is resolved by mapping to tertiary opacity (6.32:1, AA).
  - `:has()` is broadly supported (Chrome 105+, Safari 15.4+, Firefox 121+; 2023+). Allows utility classes to auto-disable when interactive content is present, satisfying "no opacity on interactive links" while keeping the system permissive for descriptive text.
  - Central tokens mean a future light-mode theme only needs new `--text-*-opacity-light` overrides without touching every selector.
- **Reversal cost**: Low-to-medium. Remove Section 16 of tokens.css + Section 11b of utilities.css + revert the 7 selectors migrated in REPORT-31. The remaining ~200+ `--text-muted`/`--text-secondary` usages would silently revert to current contrast.
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related skills**: future `design-system/text-muted-migration.md` (planned for REPORT-32) to gradually convert remaining `--text-muted` usages.
- **Related KIs**: foundational prep for KI-046 (stat label a11y; not full closure this round).


> القرارات المعمارية مع المبررات. Append-only — لا تحذف. لو قرار رُجّع، أضِف entry جديد يشير للقديم.

### DEC-001 — Tailwind disabled, vanilla CSS + tokens used instead

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: Project migrated from HTML to Astro. Tailwind was configured but caused CSS bloat and inconsistent utility usage.
- **Decision**: Disable Tailwind. Use vanilla CSS with design tokens in `src/styles/tokens.css`. Hand-written utilities in `utilities.css` for backward compatibility.
- **Rationale**: Better control, smaller CSS, consistent design system, no runtime purge complexity.
- **Reversal cost**: High (would require re-adding Tailwind, re-writing all components, re-testing all pages).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval and a full rewrite plan.
- **Related KIs**: KI-009 (Tailwind still configured but disabled — cleanup candidate).
- **Related skills**: `cleanup/dead-code-removal.md` (for removing `tailwind.config.ts` + `tailwind-merge` dep).

### DEC-002 — `trailingSlash: 'always'` in `astro.config.mjs`

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: Astro supports `'always'`, `'never'`, `'ignore'`. Existing internal links and `_redirects` use trailing slashes.
- **Decision**: `'always'`.
- **Rationale**: 314 redirects in `_redirects` assume trailing slashes. Changing breaks them all + thousands of internal links.
- **Reversal cost**: Critical (breaks 314 redirects + thousands of internal links).
- **Do not reverse. Ever.**
- **Related KIs**: KI-002 (legacy `.html` paths return 404 — `_redirects` not fully activated, separate issue).

### DEC-003 — `SplitHero.astro` is the canonical hero (serafim/splite reference)

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: User requested serafim/splite hero pattern. Multiple hero attempts existed (SparklesHero, BackgroundGrid, direct `<header>`).
- **Decision**: `SplitHero.astro` is the canonical hero. Used only in homepage. Other pages use `<PageHero variant="inner|docs|kernel" />` (to be created).
- **Rationale**: Matches the user's stated reference. Already implemented and verified.
- **Reversal cost**: Medium (would require redesigning homepage).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related components**: `src/components/SplitHero.astro` (419 lines, do not rebuild).
- **Related skills**: `design-system/page-hero-unification.md` (planned).

### DEC-004 — `DottedBackground.astro` is the global background (efferd/dotted-surface reference)

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: User requested efferd/dotted-surface background pattern.
- **Decision**: `DottedBackground.astro` loaded globally in `BaseLayout.astro:238`. Pure CSS, 0KB JS.
- **Rationale**: Matches the user's stated reference. Performance-optimal (no JS).
- **Reversal cost**: Low (single component).
- **Do not reverse** — it's the user's explicit reference.
- **Related components**: `src/components/DottedBackground.astro` (200 lines, do not rebuild).

### DEC-005 — React islands limited to visual-only effects, candidate for vanilla JS

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: React renderer (59KB gzipped) loaded on homepage only for `DottedSurface.tsx` (canvas-based dot grid with pointer ripple).
- **Decision**: Replace `DottedSurface.tsx` with vanilla JS canvas. Do not add new React islands for visual-only effects.
- **Rationale**: 59KB gzipped for a decorative canvas is unjustified. Vanilla JS can replicate the behavior in ~4KB gzipped.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (re-add React island).
- **Do not reverse** unless a future React island provides genuine interactivity that vanilla JS cannot easily replicate.
- **Related KIs**: KI-007 (React renderer loaded on homepage).
- **Related skills**: `performance/react-island-replacement.md` (ready).

### DEC-006 — `frontend/` is backend, not static

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: `frontend/` contains Express.js backend (server.js, routes, controllers, services, middleware, db, kernel logic). Published as separate Render Web Service at `brightai-api.onrender.com`.
- **Decision**: `frontend/` is backend. Do not modify when working on Astro static. Do not publish as static. Do not delete.
- **Rationale**: Separation of concerns. Backend has its own tests (`frontend/tests/`), its own deps (`frontend/package.json`), its own deployment.
- **Reversal cost**: High (would require merging backend into Astro SSR or splitting repos).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related KIs**: KI-001 (`frontend/` leaked as static — config issue, not decision issue).
- **Related skills**: `cleanup/frontend-leakage-fix.md` (planned).

### DEC-007 — Saudi dialect is the project voice (not MSA)

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: Published content uses Saudi dialect ("وش", "تبغى", "تقدر", "نبي", "يصير", "عشان"). Matches founder's voice + Saudi audience.
- **Decision**: Saudi dialect is canonical. Do not "correct" to MSA. Agent responses also in Saudi dialect (agent.md Section 0.5).
- **Rationale**: Brand consistency. Audience matching. SEO (Saudi dialect matches spoken search queries).
- **Reversal cost**: Critical (would require rewriting all published content + re-indexing in Google).
- **Do not reverse. Ever.**

### DEC-008 — `output: 'static'` in Astro (no SSR)

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: BrightAI is a content-heavy marketing + docs site. SEO is critical. Static HTML is what Google indexes best.
- **Decision**: `output: 'static'` in `astro.config.mjs`. No server-side rendering.
- **Rationale**: Best LCP/CLS/INP. Best SEO (static HTML). Cheapest hosting (Render Static). No server to maintain.
- **Reversal cost**: High (would require migration to SSR adapter, re-testing all pages, re-deploying).
- **Do not reverse** unless the project evolves to need SSR (e.g. user accounts, dynamic content).
- **Related profiles**: agent.md Section 16.1 (Astro Static Site — DEFAULT).

### DEC-009 — `public/_redirects` is the redirect source of truth

- **Date**: 2026-06-13
- **Context**: Two redirect sources existed: `public/_redirects` (314 lines) and `redirects.json` (860 lines, root).
- **Decision**: `public/_redirects` is the source. `redirects.json` is either a generator (to be verified) or dead (to be deleted).
- **Rationale**: `_redirects` is the Render/Cloudflare Pages standard. `redirects.json` is non-standard.
- **Reversal cost**: Low.
- **Related KIs**: KI-030 (unify `redirects.json` with `_redirects`).
- **Related skills**: `seo/redirect-audit.md` (planned).

### DEC-010 — Brain file at `/BRIGHTAI/.agents/brain.md`
- **Decision**: Single Markdown file at `/BRIGHTAI/.agents/brain.md`. Structure: state snapshot + change ledger + known issues + decisions log + inventory + glossary.
- **Rationale**: Simple, version-controlled (git), human-readable, no external dependencies.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (could switch to JSON or database, but Markdown is sufficient).
- **Related rules**: agent.md Section 0.6 (Brain File protocol).

### DEC-015 — Centralized scroll-reveal observer in `BaseLayout.astro`

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: 8 duplicate `IntersectionObserver` instances existed for reveal animation: one per-page in `index.astro`, `about/index.astro`, `solutions/index.astro`, `solutions/[slug].astro`, `solutions/[sector].astro`, `solutions/[sector]/[city].astro`, plus the watcher in `BaseLayout.astro` that was watching only `.reveal` (used by 0 pages). The `.feature-card`, `.card--feature`, `.inner-card` rules in `components.css` set `opacity:0` defaulting to invisible until `.is-visible` was added by JS — and the central observer didn't toggle that class, leaving cards invisible on inner pages without their own observer (a silent bug — the cards LOOKED broken to anyone visiting `solutions/[sector]/[city]/` who wasn't on the homepage).
- **Decision**: Single `IntersectionObserver` in `BaseLayout.astro` watches the union of 9 reveal-able classes (`.reveal, .feature-card, .card--feature, .inner-card, .home-stat-card, .home-faq-item, .home-evidence-mockup, .home-comparison-wrap, .inner-reveal`). Auto-attaches `--reveal-delay` CSS variable to children of any `.reveal-stagger` container (60ms per child for cascade effect). Re-runs on `astro:page-load` for view-transition page swaps. Honors `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` (flips to final state immediately) and degrades gracefully without `IntersectionObserver` support. Per-page observer scripts removed from all 6 affected pages.
- **Rationale**:
  - Single source of truth for reveal behavior → predictable across 125 pages
  - Fixes the silent invisibility bug for `.feature-card` on inner pages that had no local observer
  - Saves ~200-400 bytes per page (no more 7 duplicate observer scripts across the codebase)
  - `data-revealed` kernel-specific pattern kept in `kernel/index.astro` because it uses a custom stagger delay per-card via `data-gsap-delay` — not worth generalizing
- **Reversal cost**: Low (~5 min via `git revert`). The single observer in BaseLayout is self-contained and doesn't affect any other functionality.
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-14_PAGES-REDESIGN.md`.
- **Implementation notes**:
  - All entry-state CSS now uses `var(--reveal-delay, 0ms)` in transitions so JS-set delays work without any markup changes
  - Sections themselves are NOT revealed (always visible) — only elements with explicit entry-state CSS rules. Safer for SEO/no-JS users.
  - Kernel pages keep their own `gsap-fade[data-revealed]` pattern via `kernel/index.astro:296-310` because they use a per-card custom stagger via `data-gsap-delay` attribute
  - Future: opt-in cascade effect by wrapping any reveal-able grid in `<div class="grid reveal-stagger ...">`

### DEC-013 — 3D tilt scope = `.home-kernel-panel` only (not `.home-kernel-showcase`)

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: User requested "3D tilt خفيف على الـ kernel showcase (max 6deg)".
- **Decision**: Apply tilt to `.home-kernel-panel` (the browser-mockup card), NOT to `.home-kernel-showcase` (which includes the entire features grid below it).
- **Rationale**: The features grid (`.home-kernel-features`) is a separate visual block (own background, padding, layout). Tilt applied at `.home-kernel-showcase` level would propagate transform to the feature grid too (visually floating apart from the panel). Panel alone is the natural focal point for tilt.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (CSS selector swap + 1 JS line).
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-13_HERO-BG-POLISH.md`.

### DEC-014 — RTL fade via `:global()` ancestor scope (not `<style is:global>`)

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: RTL fade vignette required matching ancestor `[dir=rtl]` to `.dotted-bg__fade` descendant. When written naively as `[dir="rtl"] .dotted-bg__fade`, Astro scoping tightened compound selectors with `data-astro-cid-*`, producing `[data-astro-cid-XYZ][dir=rtl] .dotted-bg__fade[data-astro-cid-XYZ]` — which requires both attrs on the same element. Since `<html dir="rtl">` is a different element than `.dotted-bg`, the rule never matched.
- **Decision**: Use `:global([dir="rtl"]) .dotted-bg__fade` — wrap the ancestor selector in `:global()` so only it becomes unscoped, while `.dotted-bg__fade` remains scoped to the component.
- **Rationale**: Surgical. Preserves component encapsulation for `.dotted-bg__fade` styling while making the `[dir]` ancestor selector work regardless of which ancestor carries the data-astro-cid attribute. `<style is:global>` would force global scoping for the entire stylesheet block — overkill and would leak other unrelated selectors.
- **Reversal cost**: Trivial (drop the `:global()` prefix if scoping behavior changes in future Astro versions).
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-13_HERO-BG-POLISH.md`.

### DEC-012 — Unified Component System v4 in `components.css` (canonical + legacy aliases)

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: Project had 5 different button patterns (`.btn`, `.glow-btn`, `.wa-btn`, `.home-cta-primary`, `.home-cta-secondary`), 8 different card patterns (`.card`, `.feature-card`, `.inner-card`, `.home-stat-card`, `.home-cta-card`, `.pricing-card`, `.trust-card`, `.inner-stat`), 4 different input patterns (`.home-form-input`, `.inner-form__input`, `.form__input`, base `input`), and 3 different FAQ patterns — all achieving the same visual result with no clear standard. Same button would render in 4 different styles depending on which page.
- **Decision**: Adopt canonical BEM naming in `src/styles/components.css`:
  - Buttons: `.btn`, `.btn--primary`, `.btn--secondary`, `.btn--ghost`, `.btn--outline`, `.btn--accent`, `.btn--danger`, `.btn--glow`, `.btn--whatsapp`, `.btn--icon`, `.btn--sm`, `.btn--lg`, `.btn--block`
  - Cards: `.card`, `.card--feature`, `.card--metric`, `.card--stat`, `.card--pricing`, `.card--cta`, `.card--glass`, `.card--solid`
  - Chips/Badges: `.chip` (canonical), `.chip--brand`, `.chip--accent`, `.chip--info`, `.chip--neutral`, `.chip--success`, `.chip--warning`, `.chip--danger`
  - Sections: `.section`, `.section--alt`, `.section--dark`, `.section--inner`
  - Forms: `.field`, `.field--input`, `.field--textarea`, `.field--select`, `.field-group`, `.field-label`, `.field-help`, `.field-error`
  - Tables: `.table`, `.table--pricing`
  - FAQ: `.faq`, `.faq__item`, `.faq__answer`, `.faq__chevron`
  - Stats: `.stats`, `.stat`, `.testimonial`
  - Decorative: `.dotted-bg`, `.gradient-text`, `.section-mini-link`, `.ambient-separator`, `.glass-panel`
  - Animations: `.is-visible`, `.inner-reveal`, `.gsap-fade`

  Every legacy class name (`.glow-btn`, `.wa-btn`, `.feature-card`, `.inner-card`, etc.) is preserved as an alias using grouped selectors — same visual, no HTML changes needed.

- **Rationale**: Visual consistency across all 125 pages. Single source of truth for component styling. Easier maintenance. Future Astro components can use canonical names; legacy HTML keeps working.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (~5 min via `git revert`). Components.css and pages.css revert-safe; no downstream migration needed since aliases preserve all legacy class names.
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval. The aliases ensure zero breakage.
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-12_COMPONENTS-UNIFY.md`.
- **Implementation details**:
  - `components.css`: 585 → 1430 lines (+845)
  - `pages.css`: 2243 → 1482 lines (-761, -34%)
  - CSS gzipped (homepage BaseLayout): 14.5KB (under 25KB budget)
  - 125 pages, 0 errors, 0 broken links, 0 console errors verified
  - Zero HTML/text changes (per task constraint)

### DEC-016 — Mobile-backdrop-filter fallback for glass surfaces

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: Homepage `index.astro` has 26 instances of `.glass` class. Each triggers `backdrop-filter: blur(12px)` in `components.css:749-759`. On mobile, each instance creates a separate GPU compositing layer, costing 50-150ms TBT and 30-50ms INP.
- **Decision**: At `max-width: 767px`, drop `backdrop-filter` from `.glass, .card--glass, .glass-panel` and use opaque background `rgba(15, 21, 37, 0.92)`. Text legibility preserved.
- **Rationale**: Mobile devices have limited GPU memory. Opaque fallback sacrifices the "frosted glass" look (acceptable per design intent) for -50-150ms TBT and -30-50ms INP. Desktop unchanged. Does not affect DottedBackground (already mobile-tuned separately).
- **Reversal cost**: Trivial (3 lines in @media).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related KIs**: KI-043 (26+ glass on mobile), KI-044 (3 backdrop-filters in SplitHero).
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-15_MOBILE-AND-3D.md`.

### DEC-017 — `client:media` gates React island on mobile (KI-007 escalation)

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: `DottedSurface` React island in `SplitHero.astro:36` uses `client:visible`. This delays hydration but does NOT prevent script download. React renderer (58KB gzipped) loads on mobile even though the component is mobile-gated internally (`isMobile` check at `DottedSurface.tsx:74`).
- **Decision**: Change to `client:visible client:media="(min-width: 768px)"`. Astro will not inject the `<script type="module">` for the island on viewports <768px. Aligns with the existing 3-tier mobile gating pattern (3D tilt, lowPower canvas, skip pointer).
- **Rationale**: LCP improvement ~80-150ms, INP ~50ms, payload -58KB gzipped on mobile. Matches the 768px breakpoint already used elsewhere (DottedBackground, SplitHero, components.css).
- **Reversal cost**: Trivial (1 word in SplitHero.astro).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related KIs**: KI-007 (React renderer loaded — escalated), KI-042 (specific instance).
- **Related skills**: `performance/react-island-replacement.md` (broader goal: replace DottedSurface entirely with vanilla JS; this is the intermediate fix).

### DEC-018 — Stat label minimum 12px (WCAG 2.2 AA compliance)

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: `.home-stat-card__label` and `.inner-stat__label` use 10px on mobile (`components.css:553-556` and `pages.css:170`). WCAG 2.2 minimum readable text is 11px; 10px is below. Affects 3 stat cards on homepage.
- **Decision**: Bump to `var(--text-xs)` = 12px on mobile. Single canonical rule in `components.css:555`; remove duplicate override in `pages.css:170`. Contrast ratio on `--bg-base` with `--text-muted` (#94a3b8) at 12px = ~4.6:1, passes AA.
- **Rationale**: Accessibility + readability. Above-the-fold stat labels are read frequently. 12px is the standard Tailwind `text-xs` size and the de facto minimum for production sites.
- **Reversal cost**: Trivial (1 line change + 3 lines removed).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related KIs**: KI-046.

### DEC-041 — Mobile Hierarchy Runbook (REPORT-33)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: Homepage hero on mobile (≤768px) had 4 buttons with equal prominence (wa-btn + home-cta-primary + 2 secondary all same font-size/weight), hero primary CTA at y=902px (off the 844px fold), React renderer downloading 58KB gzipped JS for a decoration that was display:hidden anyway, and secondary text links falling below WCAG 2.5.5's 44px tap target (AAA).
- **Decision**:
  1. **Tap-target enforcement** — global CSS rule in `src/styles/components.css`:
     `.wa-btn, .home-cta-primary, .home-cta-secondary, .home-kernel-action, .section-mini-link { min-height: 44px; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }`.
  2. **Mobile CTA hierarchy** — `@media (max-width: 768px)` in same file:
     - `.home-hero-actions { flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; }`
     - WhatsApp (`order: 1`) + Book Demo (`order: 2`) at 56px tall + full-width
     - 2 secondary CTAs (`order: 3, 4`) at 48px tall + smaller font
  3. **React island gate** — `client:visible` → `client:visible client:media="(min-width: 768px)"` on `<DottedSurface>` in `SplitHero.astro:39`. Verified: scripts=0 on mobile viewport.
  4. **SSR canvas hidden on mobile** — global CSS rule in `components.css`:
     `.dotted-surface-canvas { display: none !important; }`
     This rule MUST live in global CSS because the React island's SSR-rendered canvas does not carry the SplitHero `data-astro-cid` attribute, so Astro's scoped styles never match it. (Same lesson as DEC-014.)
  5. **Mobile compaction in SplitHero.astro** — star 230→150px, lead `-webkit-line-clamp: 3` to preserve CTA above fold, kernel-feature cells 84px (was ~58px).
- **Rationale**:
  - **Council synthesis (5 lenses)**: Red-Team rejected sticky bars. Pragmatic kept change set to ~85 lines CSS + 1 HTML attr. A11y enforced WCAG 2.5.5 AAA. Performance killed the React renderer on mobile. Brand preserved "The Star" at compact size.
  - **Mobile-first visual chain** (390×844): header (88px) + chip (~30) + star (≤120) + H1 (≤120) + lead (≤90) + CTAs (≥224) = ~672px → 170px breathing room before fold.
  - **Verification**: Playwright audit `scripts/playwright/verify-mobile-hierarchy.mjs` runs 7 acceptance checks on iPhone 14 viewport. Result: 7/7 pass, `audit.json: { ok: true, failures: [] }`.
- **Reversal cost**: Trivial. Revert 2 source files (components.css, SplitHero.astro) + delete 1 verification script. Total ~85 lines of CSS to remove.
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related KIs**: KI-007 (resolved), KI-042 (resolved). KI-043/044 (mobile-backdrop-filter) untouched — separate ticket.
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-33_MOBILE-HIERARCHY.md` (~500 lines).

### DEC-042 — GEO Surface (Seed Vocabulary + Pillars + Coverage + AEO) in llms.txt / llms-full.txt

- **Date**: 2026-07-08
- **Context**: BrightAI ينافس على AI Overviews / ChatGPT / Perplexity citations للمنافسة في السوق السعودي للـ AI governance. الـ llms.txt/llms-full.txt كانت تحوي positioning + nav فقط — ما كانت تعرّف الـ semantic space اللي AI engine يربط استعلامات المستخدمين فيها. بدون seed vocabulary + pillar anchors + AEO questions، إجابات LLMs تاخذ generic content من `/about/` و `/services/` بدل pillar authority pages.
- **Decision**: إضافة 4 أقسام ثابته في `public/llms.txt` و `public/llms-full.txt` (بعد بنية موجودة، بدون حذف):
  1. **Seed Vocabulary** — 5 keywords سعودية مع تعريفات neutral بالإنجليزي: حوكمة الذكاء الاصطناعي، نظام حماية البيانات الشخصية، ضوابط الامن السيبراني (NCA ECC)، إرشادات سدايا للذكاء الاصطناعي التوليدي، شركات الذكاء الاصطناعي في السعودية.
  2. **Topical Pillars** — 6 pillar URLs موجودة فعلياً في `src/content/blog/`, `src/content/docs/`, `src/pages/solutions/`: ai-governance-saudi-arabia, pdpl-ai-complete-guide, nca-ecc-ai-controls, sdaia-generative-ai-guidelines, /solutions, iso-42001-saudi-implementation-guide.
  3. **Topical Coverage** — 6 عناقيد بجملة لكل عنقود: Governance، PDPL، NCA ECC، SDAIA، Sector Solutions، ISO 42001.
  4. **AEO Question Bank** — 6 أسئلة بصياغة المستخدم السعودية الكاملة، مع ربط كل سؤال بـ pillar URL في llms-full.txt (llms.txt يحوي الأسئلة بدون ربط لتخفيف الحجم).
- **Rationale**:
  1. **Pillar authority matching**: AI engines تحب pillar URL واحد يجاوب على query كامل. ربط السؤال بالـ pillar URL يعطي الـ LLM citation جاهز.
  2. **Neutral English definitions**: الـ seed vocabulary لازم يكون machine-parseable للـ LLMs الأجنبية. لو حطيناه بالعربي dialect خام، Perplexity/Claude ممكن يفهموه غلط.
  3. **Saudi dialect questions**: الـ AEO bank يعكس user-intent حقيقي (وش يسأل السعودي) — هنا السعودي dialect واجب لأن هذي نسخة user.
  4. **No removals**: الأقسام الأربعة additive فقط. ما حذفنا ولا section ولا أعدنا تسمية.
- **Reversal cost**: Trivial. احذف الأقسام الأربعة من الملفين (سطر 95–124 في llms.txt، سطر 121–158 في llms-full.txt). البناء يظل يمر لأن الأقسام خارج Astro rendering pipeline (public/ ينسخ كما هو).
- **Do not reverse** بدون user approval — الأقسام صارت contract مع AI engines، حذفها يفقد citation surface.
- **Related**: Skill `seo-geo` (GEO methodology)؛ report: `report/2026-07-08-geo-surface-upgrade.md` (لو احتجناه لاحقاً).

### DEC-022 — THE STAR — single SVG brand identity

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: BrightAI يفتقر لـ "signature visual" يربط البراند بصرياً عبر الموقع. الـ hero text يحكي القصة لكن كل صفحة كانت تستخدم رموز منفصلة (BackgroundGrid للأقسام الداخلية، emoji/☁️/🛡️ في الـ spots، logo.png التقليدي في الـ chrome). هذا يخلق brand fragmentation.
- **Decision**: اعتماد **The Star** — SVG signature موحّد يجسّد المعمار (الموظف / Kernel Shield / AI+Data). خمسة variants: `hero` (full 3-layer storytelling in homepage)، `minimal` (footer/header mark)، `mono` (broken connection لـ 404)، `glow`/`loading` (loading state). Brand gradient (#22d3ee → #818cf8) و hex shield silhouette + lock glyph في كل variant. Pure SVG — 0KB JS، CSS-only animations، `prefers-reduced-motion` safe.
- **Rationale**:
  1. **Coherence**: لمسة بصرية واحدة عبر كل touchpoint (hero, footer, 404, loading).
  2. **Performance**: SVG inline = 1.67KB gz (hero)، ≤1KB gz (rest). مقابل logo.png كان يأخذ request إضافي.
  3. **A11y**: aria-hidden على الـ decorative hero star، role="img" + aria-label على standalone SVGs.
  4. **RTL-safe**: viewBox + symmetric coordinates، مافي left/right literals.
  5. **Lighthouse-friendly**: 0KB JS added، no FOUC، no hydration cost.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (revert 6 files: SplitHero, Footer, 404, pages.css, delete 5 SVGs + 2 components).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval — هذا الـ "brand DNA الموحّد" المرتبط بكل صفحة.
- **Related report**: `report/REPORT-28_STAR-VISUAL.md`.

### DEC-019 — Canonical Organization @id reused across the site

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: لتعزيز E-E-A-T + cross-page entity resolution، نحتاج Organization node واحد يشار إليه من كل صفحة.
- **Decision**: استخدام `@id: https://brightai.site/#organization` كـ reference point ثابت. كل صفحة تستورد `ORGANIZATION` من `src/data/schema-helpers.ts` ويصدره كـ node في `@graph`. الـ node يحوي: founder (Person), address, contactPoint, areaServed (7 areas), knowsAbout (12 topics), knowsLanguage, slogan, brand.
- **Rationale**: Google يحبذ canonical entity references. يشير للسلطة المؤسسية الموحدة + يدعم Knowledge Graph eligibility.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (revert schema-helpers.ts).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval.
- **Related**: DEC-020 (LocalBusiness), DEC-021 (HowTo).
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-23_SCHEMA-ENHANCEMENT.md`.

### DEC-020 — LocalBusiness per-city enrichment عبر 6 canonical nodes

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: الموقع يخدم 6 مدن سعودية. الرئيسية وkernel/index يصدّران الآن 6 LocalBusiness nodes مع GeoCoordinates + GeoCircle + Wikidata IDs.
- **Decision**: 6 LocalBusiness canonical nodes (Riyadh, Jeddah, Dammam, Khobar, Mecca, Madinah) تُصدّر من الرئيسية + kernel/index، وكل city page يصدّر LocalBusiness الخاص بها. العقد يحوي: address + geo + serviceArea (GeoCircle 100km) + OpeningHoursSpecification + paymentAccepted + currenciesAccepted + knowsAbout + provider/parentOrganization cross-references.
- **Rationale**: يدعم Local Pack eligibility + "Near Me" search (~30% Saudi searches). Google يحبذ LocalBusiness على مستوى المدينة مع geo coords.
- **Reversal cost**: Low.
- **Related KIs**: KI-005 (resolved 2026-06-29).
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-23_SCHEMA-ENHANCEMENT.md`.

### DEC-021 — HowTo schema عبر DocsLayout + 2 procedural pages

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: صفحات docs طويلة بدون HowTo schema. يحتاج المشروع HowTo rich result eligibility لصفحات الإرشادات التطبيقية.
- **Decision**: HowTo schema مع `step[]` array لكل وثيقة. DocsLayout يصدّر HowTo generic (3 steps: افهم → طبّق → وثّق). docs/index يصدّر HowTo محدد "خطة 30 يوم" (6 steps + P30D duration). assessment يصدّر HowTo "تقييم 4 خطوات" (4 steps + P14D duration). Steps generic لتجنب fabrication.
- **Rationale**: HowTo qualifies for rich results. يضيف semantic structure للوثائق + يدعم AI answer engines (GEO).
- **Reversal cost**: Low.
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-23_SCHEMA-ENHANCEMENT.md`.

### DEC-011 — Removed `frontend/` (Express backend + static assets) entirely

- **Date**: 2026-06-29
- **Context**: User decided to drop the `frontend/` directory entirely because the site fully migrated from HTML to Astro. The `frontend/` contained both an Express backend (`server.js`, routes, controllers, services, middleware, db, kernel logic) AND static assets (CSS, JS, fonts, images) that the static HTML site used to reference.
- **Decision**: Delete `frontend/` completely. Remove backend deps from `package.json`. Remove `brightai-api` web service and `brightai-db` database from `render.yaml`. Remove `/api/*` and `/ws/*` rewrites. Add hard block headers + redirect for `/frontend/*` as defense in depth.
- **Rationale**: Astro static site no longer needs the Express backend. The HTML in `dist/` does not reference `/frontend/*` paths (verified via grep on `dist/`). All static assets are now produced by Astro into `dist/_astro/`, `dist/assets/`, and `dist/fonts/`.
- **Reversal cost**: Medium (~15 minutes via `git revert` of all related commits + restore `frontend/` from git history + `npm install`).
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval and a feature requirement (e.g. needing AI gateway, kernel backend, or database-driven features).
- **Related KIs**: KI-001 (frontend/ leakage → RESOLVED by this decision).
- **Related reports**: `report/REPORT-FRONTEND-REMOVAL-2026-06-29.md`.
- **Related risks**:
  - Cloudflare CDN cache may serve stale `/frontend/*` files for TTL after deploy. Mitigation: post-deploy manual purge.
  - `/kernel/*` pages that previously fetched from `/api/*` will now fail. Follow-up ticket needed to refactor or remove kernel API calls.

### DEC-2026-LINKS-001 — LocalBusiness nodes cite existing sector pages (`/solutions/<sector>/<city>/`)

- **Date**: 2026-07-07
- **Context**: `LOCAL_BUSINESS_BY_CITY` كان يولّد `${SITE.url}/solutions/${citySlug}/` لكل مدينة (riyadh/mecca/madinah/jeddah/dammam/khobar) في `dist/` لكن `/solutions/<city>/` alone ما موجود — الصفحات الفعلية تحت `/solutions/<sector>/<city>/` (مثل `/solutions/banking-ai-governance/riyadh/`). يعني 18 رابط مكسور حقيقي في `dist/` (3 * 6 مدن) من JSON-LD داخل 4 صفحات (index, about, kernel, demo).
- **Decision**: `getLocalBusinessLd(input)` يقبل `href: string` كمعطى إجباري. كل city يمرّر href ثابت يطابق الـ sector landing الفعلي (riyadh + khobar → banking، dammam + madinah → government، jeddah + mecca → healthcare). الـcanonical في JSON-LD يُبنى من `${SITE.url}${href}`.
- **Rationale**: يضمن أن كل LocalBusiness @id يدل على صفحة فعلية indexable من Google. الـschema المحلي يصبح صالح للـLocal Pack بدون false signals.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (revert schema-helpers.ts).
- **Do not change sector-city mapping** بدون تحديث both src/data/solutions.ts (canonical) و src/data/schema-helpers.ts (city href) معاً — وإلا يصير mismatch جديد.
- **Related reports**: `reports/2026-07-07-internal-links-zero.md`.

### DEC-2026-LINKS-002 — link-graph-validator scans `dist/` + `public/`, not project root

- **Date**: 2026-07-07
- **Context**: `scripts/link-graph-validator.mjs` كان يستخدم `walkFiles(PROJECT_ROOT)` فيمسح dist/, kernel/, qa-*.mjs, _reports/, _archive/ — ينتج 18777 false positive broken (relPathToRoute يحفظ routes بـ prefix `dist/` لكن hrefs canonicalized بدونه). كذلك ما يحسب XML routes (rss.xml, sitemap.xml) — يفترض HTML فقط.
- **Decision**: السكربت يفحص dist/ + public/ فقط (سطح النشر الفعلي)، يستثني 404.html/500.html/error.html من broken-link check (نوايا error page هي قائمة بكل routes)، ويحفظ XML/well-known assets كـ routes إضافية (rss.xml, sitemap*.xml, manifest.webmanifest, robots.txt, llms.txt, humans.txt, ai.txt).
- **Rationale**: فحص dist/public فقط = فحص الـ artifact المنشور الفعلي → صفر false positive. دعم XML routes = sitemap وRSS في dist جزء من النشر (يتصفحها المستخدم ومتصفح Google)، يستحق أن يكون route صالح.
- **Reversal cost**: Low (revert script).
- **Do not reverse** بدون التأكد أن ROOT الافتراضي ما يلوّث routes بـ prefixes أو ينتج noisy outputs.
- **Related reports**: `reports/2026-07-07-internal-links-zero.md`.

## 6. Glossary

- **Saudi Voice**: The project's voice is Saudi dialect (عامية سعودية). See agent.md Section 0.5. Do not "correct" to MSA.
- **Brain File**: This file. `/BRIGHTAI/.agents/brain.md`. Persistent cross-session memory.
- **Worklog**: Per-session task log. `/home/z/my-project/worklog.md`. Append-only within a session.
- **Report**: Detailed per-change documentation. `report/YYYY-MM-DD-description.md`. Permanent.
- **Skill**: Reusable procedure. `/BRIGHTAI/.agents/skills/<category>/<name>.md`. Versioned.
- **Protected files**: Files that cannot be modified without explicit user approval. See agent.md Section 2.1.
- **Dead code**: Code with 0 usages (verified via `grep -rn "ComponentName" src/`). Safe to remove after snapshot.
- **Blast radius**: The number of files/pages affected by a change. Low = <5 files. Medium = 5-10. High = 10+ or all pages.
- **90% confidence rule**: The agent does not execute a change unless confidence ≥ 90%. See agent.md Section 0.7.2.
- **Non-trivial change**: Touching 2+ files, or modifying a protected file, or any change that affects build/SEO/performance. Requires brain.md read before + brain.md write after.
- **Trivial change**: Typo fix in a comment, formatting only, single-file edit with no build impact. Requires brain.md read before, no write after.
- **Large task**: 10+ files, or 2+ hours, or 3+ domains. Requires phased plan + user approval before execution. See agent.md Section 0.7.6.
- **Tier 1 file**: Critical path file, read fully at session start. See agent.md Section 3.1.
- **Tier 2 file**: Task-adjacent file, read fully if task is in its domain.
- **Tier 3 file**: Reference file, read specific sections on demand only.
- **Canonical component**: A component that is the authoritative implementation. Do not rebuild. See Section 5.3.
- **Deprecated component**: A component marked for removal. See Section 5.4.
- **Design token**: A CSS custom property defined in `src/styles/tokens.css`. See Section 5.1.
- **Compliance Pack**: A regulatory bundle (PDPL, NCA, SFDA, SAMA, ZATCA, ISO 13485, ISO 42001). Referenced in `featureList` schema.
- **Kernel**: BrightAI's runtime governance engine. 11 pages under `/kernel/*`. Backend logic in `frontend/kernel/`.
- **Saudi AI Safety OS**: BrightAI's tagline. The product is an OS-like layer between employees and AI models.
- **PDPL**: Personal Data Protection Law (نظام حماية البيانات الشخصية). Saudi regulation.
- **NCA ECC**: National Cybersecurity Authority Essential Cybersecurity Controls. Saudi regulation.
- **SDAIA**: Saudi Data and AI Authority. Issues generative AI guidelines.
- **SFDA**: Saudi Food and Drug Authority. Regulates medical devices + pharmaceuticals.
- **SAMA**: Saudi Central Bank. Regulates banking.
- **ZATCA**: Zakat, Tax and Customs Authority.
- **IndexNow**: Protocol for instant search engine notification. Key at `public/e158df443f2742d281a02c4aeecb4a60.txt`.
- **View Transitions**: Astro's client-side navigation. Loaded via `ClientRouter` in `BaseLayout.astro:16`.
- **React island**: A React component hydrated client-side via `@astrojs/react`. Only `DottedSurface.tsx` currently. Candidate for vanilla JS replacement (DEC-005).
- **`trailingSlash: 'always'`**: Astro config that forces trailing slashes on all URLs. Do not change (DEC-002).
- **`output: 'static'`**: Astro config for static site generation. Do not change (DEC-008).
- **CSP**: Content Security Policy. Defined in `public/_headers`. Allows specific domains for scripts, styles, fonts, connections.
- **HSTS**: HTTP Strict Transport Security. Forces HTTPS. `max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains; preload`.
- **X-Robots-Tag**: HTTP header controlling indexing. Currently `index, follow, max-image-preview:large`.

## 9. Verification Commands Cheat Sheet

> أوامر التحقق السريعة. الوكيل يشغلها قبل claim "done".

### Build & verify
```bash
npm run build               # → 125 pages, 0 errors
npm run verify:all          # → exit 0
npm run seo:all             # → exit 0 (if SEO-related)
npm run performance:budget  # → exit 0 (if CSS/JS-related)
npm run internal-links:audit # → exit 0
npm run redirects:check     # → exit 0
npm run sitemap:generate    # → updates public/sitemap.xml
npm run image-sitemap       # → updates public/sitemap-images.xml
```

### Post-deploy curl tests
```bash
curl -I https://brightai.site/                                    # 200
curl -I https://brightai.site/about.html                          # 301 (not 404!)
curl -I https://brightai.site/frontend/server.js                  # 404 (not 200!)
curl -I https://brightai.site/sitemap.xml                         # 200
curl -I https://brightai.site/robots.txt                          # 200
curl -I https://brightai.site/e158df443f2742d281a02c4aeecb4a60.txt # 200
```

### Playwright verification
```bash
npx playwright install chromium  # one-time
node scripts/playwright/measure-vitals.mjs --url=http://localhost:4321/ --device=mobile-slow-4g
node scripts/playwright/capture-console-errors.mjs --url=http://localhost:4321/
node scripts/playwright/screenshot.mjs --url=http://localhost:4321/ --viewport=390x844 --output=download/qa/homepage-mobile.png
node scripts/playwright/axe-audit.mjs --url=http://localhost:4321/
```

### IndexNow (post-deploy)
```bash
npm run indexnow:trigger  # notifies Bing/Yandex
```

### DEC-2026-034 — Card System Unification (canonical + legacy aliases)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: 191 استخدام لـ `.inner-card` و24 استخدام لـ `.home-stat-card` بدون نظام موحّد للـvariants والـsizes والـanatomy. كل صفحة تسوي بطاقات بطريقتها، والـcode يكرر نفسه. الـSolutionCard و SectorCard متطابقين 100% (drift قديم).
- **Decision**: نظام موحّد تحت `.card` base يتكون من: (1) 6 variants — feature/metric/glass/gradient(interactive)/solid، (2) 3 sizes — sm/md/lg، (3) 6 anatomy sub-elements — icon/eyebrow/title/desc/meta/action، (4) trend indicator للـmetric، (5) 10 legacy aliases preserved indefinitely (HTML compatibility — 191+ usages unchanged).
- **Rationale**: يحقق 4 أهداف — (1) visual consistency عبر الموقع، (2) code reusability (الـSolutionCard refactored من 63 → 50 سطر، يقلل التكرار)، (3) design system documentation (`/design/cards/` internal preview page)، (4) future flexibility (الـvariants الجديدة gradient + interactive تفتح إمكانيات تصميم).
- **Reversal cost**: Medium. الـbase `.card` style يمكن إعادة تعريفه. الـlegacy aliases تحمي 191+ استخدام. الـSolutionCard/SectorCard refactor يحتاج إعادة اختبار.
- **Do not reverse** aliases to canonical — الـlegacy HTML في الإنتاج يجب أن يظل يعمل بدون تعديل.
- **Related decisions**: DEC-2026-033 (color system — uses tokens throughout), DEC-012 (unified component system v4 — this extends it).
- **Related reports**: `reports/07-cards.md` (~500 lines, Saudi dialect, full breakdown + decision log + risks).
- **Risks**:
  - Page-specific overrides على `.inner-card` (مثل inline gradient backgrounds في `/solutions/index.astro`) — موجودة لكن local-scoped، لا تؤثر على النظام الموحّد.
  - SolutionCard و SectorCard متطابقين بصرياً — kept separate intentionally for typed API.

### DEC-2026-035 — Buttons + Forms System Unification (REPORT-08)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: 81 button usage بدون size ladder موحّد (كان فقط sm/lg، ناقص md/xl). لا loading state (forms يستخدمون custom inline spinner أو بدون). Icon buttons بدون variants للأحجام. Focus ring متفاوت (كل variant عنده ring مختلف أو بدون). Forms تستخدم 4 patterns مختلفة (`.field`/`.home-form-input`/`.inner-form__input`/`.inner-form__group`) مع styles ناقصة — `.inner-form__group/label/submit` مستخدمة في contact.astro بدون تنسيق CSS. لا preview page للـ buttons أو forms.
- **Decision**: نظام موحّد تحت: (1) **Buttons** — `.btn` base + 8 variants (primary/secondary/ghost/outline/whatsapp/danger/glow/accent) + 4 sizes (sm 36 / md 44 default / lg 52 / xl 60) + 5 states (hover/active/focus/disabled/loading) + `.btn--icon` بـ 3 size variants + CSS-only spinner للـ loading + unified focus ring 2px offset 2px brand color، (2) **Forms** — `.input` canonical + 3 sizes (sm/md/lg) + 5 variants (textarea/select/checkbox/radio/file) + 5 layout helpers (`.form-field`/`.form-row`/`.form-label`/`.form-helper`/`.form-error`) + 6 states (default/hover/focus/disabled/readonly/error)، (3) **Preview pages** — `/design/buttons/` بـ 7 sections و `/design/forms/` بـ 11 sections + جدول aliases، (4) **14 legacy aliases preserved indefinitely** للـ HTML compatibility (`.field`/`.inner-form__*`/`.home-form-input`/`.btn-primary`/`.btn-secondary`/`.btn-ghost`/`.btn-accent`/`.glow-btn`/`.home-cta-primary`/`.home-cta-secondary`/`.wa-btn`/`.field-group`/`.field-label`/`.field-help`/`.field-error`).
- **Rationale**: يحقق 5 أهداف — (1) **size ladder موحّد** (44px default → AAA، 60px hero → أكبر CTAs)، (2) **loading state مع CSS-only spinner** (لا JS، متوافق مع reduced-motion)، (3) **focus ring موحّد** عبر كل الـ variants (يحل مشكلة الـ a11y)، (4) **5 form layout helpers** (`.form-field`/`.form-row`/`.form-label`/`.form-helper`/`.form-error` = نظام كامل بـ 5 عناصر)، (5) **preview pages** للـ design system documentation. الـ legacy aliases تحمي 30+ عنصر موجود بدون تعديل HTML.
- **Reversal cost**: Low. الـcanonical + aliases setup forward-compatible. إضافة sizes جديدة أو variants أخرى = append-only edits في components.css. الـ preview pages منفصلة (تحت `/design/*` مستثنى من sitemap).
- **Do not reverse** aliases to canonical — الـlegacy HTML في الإنتاج يجب أن يظل يعمل بدون تعديل.
- **Related decisions**: DEC-2026-033 (color system — focus ring uses --interactive-primary token), DEC-2026-034 (card system — preview page pattern reused), DEC-SovB-001 (Deep Teal brand color), DEC-012 (unified component system v4 — this extends it).
- **Related reports**: `reports/08-buttons-forms.md` (~700 lines, Saudi dialect, 12 sections, full breakdown + verification + risk analysis + commit message + post-deploy checklist).
- **Risks**:
  - `.btn--sm` (36px) is below WCAG 2.5.5 AAA — intentional for dense UI rows, documented in Section 5.7 of report (3/4 sizes meet AAA).
  - `.inner-form__submit` is custom (not `.btn.btn--primary`) — slight duplication, justified by form-specific needs (type=submit + full-width on mobile).
  - Homepage lead form (`index.astro:537`) still uses Tailwind classes for inputs — documented in Section 8.3 as follow-up (constraint said "لا تغيّر أي form موجود في الـ pages").
  - 14 legacy aliases preserved indefinitely — new code should use canonical names (`.btn--primary` over `.btn-primary`).
- **Aliases for follow-up migration** (low priority, 1 day):
  - `.glow-btn` → `.btn--glow` (32+ usages)
  - `.wa-btn` → `.btn--whatsapp` (12+ usages)
  - `.home-cta-primary` → `.btn--primary.btn--glow` (homepage hero)
  - `.home-cta-secondary` → `.btn--secondary` (homepage hero)

### DEC-2026-041 — Kernel Shell Pattern (REPORTS-14)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: 11 صفحة Kernel (chat, audit, approvals, evidence, stats, scenarios, compliance, policies, connectors, reports, offline) كانت بدون shell layout موحد — كل صفحة عندها markup مختلف، visual style مختلف، تكامل API مختلف. الـ user direction النهائي: "ارجاع `/kernel/` لما قبل التغيير، وانقل كل الخصائص والتكاملات إلى Astro" — يعني لا HTML في `/kernel/`, كل شيء في Astro.
- **Decision**: وحّدنا الـ shell تحت canonical pattern مع 11 Astro components جديدة:
  - **Sidebar**: `.kernel-sidebar` (fixed position على اليمين RTL، 260px ↔ 72px collapsed، localStorage persisted). على mobile: hidden + drawer من KernelLayout. Components: `KernelSidebar.astro` (175 سطر، يحوي collapse JS + aria-labels).
  - **Page header**: `.kernel-page-header` (title + subtitle + actions row، breadcrumb via `.kernel-breadcrumb`).
  - **Metric cards**: `.kernel-metric-card` (label + trend badge + value بـ gradient text variants brand/gold + unit + footer). variants: up/down/flat trend.
  - **Status strip**: `.kernel-status-strip` (lead dot + 3 meta items، responsive).
  - **Activity stream**: `.kernel-activity-list` + `.kernel-activity-item` (icon + title + trace-id LTR داخل RTL + meta + time، tones: default/success/warning/danger).
  - **Risk chart**: `.kernel-risk-chart` + `.kernel-risk-row` (5 levels: minimal/low/medium/high/critical بـ colored bars).
  - **Tabs**: `.kernel-tabs` (pill-style + count badges + active underline).
  - **Empty + skeleton**: `.kernel-empty` (illustrated + CTA) + `.kernel-skeleton` (7 variants للـ shimmer).
  - **Demo pill**: `.kernel-demo-pill` (Copper accent-warm بـ DEC-SovB-004، aria-pressed toggle، emits `kernel:demo-toggle` event).
- **Rationale**:
  - **No `/kernel/*.html`** — كل شيء في Astro. build pipeline موحد، view transitions events جاهزة، components reusable.
  - **Sidebar position fixed** (مو grid) عشان يشتغل بغض النظر عن DOM nesting (الـ sidebar nested داخل BaseLayout's `<div class="page">` بسبب slot).
  - **V3 tokens فقط** (DEC-SovB-001 Teal، DEC-SovB-004 Copper) — لا raw rgba إلا في shadows.
  - **Logical properties** لـ RTL: `margin-inline-start`, `padding-inline-end`, `inset-inline-end`.
  - **Keyboard shortcuts** preserved من PWA (C/A/S/T/R) — يعمل عبر `astro:page-load`.
  - **Mobile drawer** من KernelLayout inline JS — نشط على <1024px بدون JS module جديد.
  - **0 published Arabic text modified** — كل النصوص من `kernelUnits` + `dashboardMetrics` + `activityEvents` data files rendered verbatim.
- **Reversal cost**: متوسطة. الـ 11 components في `src/components/kernel/` سهلة الحذف. الـ CSS shell block في `src/styles/kernel.css` (~1370 سطر) قابل للعزل. لو حبينا نرجع لـ inline markup، لازم نمسح الـ components ونعيد توزيع الـ markup في كل صفحة — سهل لكن متعب.
- **Do not build bespoke Kernel dashboard widgets** بدون استخدام الـ canonical components. لو احتجت widget جديد، أضف component جديد بدلاً من inline.

### DEC-2026-042 — Kernel Interactive Pages Pattern (REPORTS-15)

- **Date**: 2026-07-06
- **Context**: بعد REPORTS-14، صفحات `/kernel/chat/`, `/audit/`, `/approvals/`, `/evidence/` كانت read-only (status strip + empty state + قائمة seed mock بدون أي تفاعل). الـ user direction: "وش النسوي ذا — صفحة chat محادثة؟!" — أي الـ pages تحتاج enterprise dashboard interactivity كاملة.
- **Decision**: أضفنا canonical interactive patterns للـ 4 pages (vanilla JS — لا new deps):
  - **3-pane chat layout** (`.kernel-chat-layout` بـ CSS Grid `260px / 1fr / 320px` على desktop، stacked على mobile <1024px): threads list (left، `data-kernel-threads`)، chat thread (center، bubbles RTL مع brand bg للـ user / surface للـ AI، `.kernel-chat-message--user` بـ `align-self: flex-end`، `.kernel-chat-message--ai` بـ `align-self: flex-start`، avatars دائرية 28px، `border-end-end-radius` صغير للـ user، `border-end-start-radius` صغير للـ AI)، context panel (right، `.kernel-chat-context-card` لـ NVIDIA NIM مع 3 fields: status / model / mode + policy alerts + session meta).
  - **Bubble streaming simulator** (vanilla `setInterval` 28ms/tok): user يضغط send → user bubble ينضاف → typing indicator (3 dots بـ `kernelChatTyping` keyframes، delays 0/0.15/0.30s) → بعد latency 700ms → pick fake response من array → `streamInto` يكتبها word-by-word مع support لروابط HTML. الـ Enter يرسل، Shift+Enter سطر جديد.
  - **Audit filter + table + drawer pattern** (`.kernel-audit-filters` × 5 fields + `.kernel-audit-table` sticky-header + `.kernel-audit-drawer` slides from inline-end بـ `translateX`، في RTL `translateX(-100%)`، 350ms `var(--ease-out)`): risk badges 4-tone (low/medium/high/critical) بـ rgba backgrounds، hash cell (`.kernel-audit-table__hash` + monospace + truncate 12 chars + ... + 4 chars + `.kernel-audit-table__hash-copy` بـ `navigator.clipboard.writeText` + textarea fallback). الـ filters live-update count + visibility.
  - **Approval tabs + cards + modal pattern** (`.kernel-approval-card` بـ `border-inline-start 4px solid <status>` للـ critical/high/medium + `.kernel-modal` overlay + dialog مع signature/comment textarea + reject/approve buttons، reject يتطلب reason): `.kernel-approvals-tabs-count--alert` بيـ pulse على الـ pending tab (2s loop). Post-decision: card fade-out 350ms → count updates → re-bind للـ cards المتبقية.
  - **Evidence grid + preview + generate pattern** (`.kernel-evidence-grid` auto-fill `minmax(280px, 1fr)` + `.kernel-evidence-item` بـ icon + title monospace + meta dl + signature status + buttons + preview modal `.kernel-modal__iframe` بـ srcdoc ديناميكي للـ brand-aligned HTML preview + Generate New button يبني `<article>` element in DOM، `grid.insertBefore(article, firstChild)`, fade-in animation، re-binds events).
- **Rationale**:
  - **No new dependencies** — كل التفاعل vanilla JS (EventTarget.addEventListener، setInterval، navigator.clipboard.writeText، URL.createObjectURL، document.execCommand fallback). Chat streaming عبر token-by-token setInterval ما يحتاج الـ OpenAI/Claude SSE حقيقي (الفرق)، demo mode يحاكي نفس الـ UX.
  - **Drawer's RTL-aware** بـ `[dir="rtl"]` selector للحالة الابتدائية (`translateX(-100%)` بدل `100%`) — يفتح من اليمين في RTL، يسار في LTR.
  - **iframe srcdoc** بدل external PDF route — يشتغل offline، brand-aligned بصرياً، zero load.
  - **Generate button live DOM inserts** — يستخدم `grid.insertBefore` + animation class — يحاكي نفس الـ pattern اللي الـ API الحقيقي يـستخدمه.
  - **Modal stacking**: الـ approval modal و الـ evidence preview modal كلاهما `z-index: 90`، drawer تحت 80. لا تداخل functional في الـ flows الحالية، لكن إذا في flow يحتاج اثنين مفتوحين، نرفع الـ preview modal للـ 95.
  - **CSS append-only** — sections 22–29 ضيفت قبل `print` media query. الحفاظ على الـ baseline shell pattern من REPORTS-14 (sections 9–21).
- **Reversal cost**: منخفضة. الـ CSS جديد prefix بـ `kernel-chat-*`, `kernel-audit-*`, `kernel-approval-*`, `kernel-evidence-*` — ما يتداخل مع الـ baseline. الـ Astro pages الجديدة تحل محل الـ 4 versions القديمة. `git revert` ينظف كل شي.
- **Do not rebuild interactive kernels** — استخدم الـ patterns. لو احتجت صفحة kernel جديدة بـ interactivity، ابني على نفس الـ CSS classes + data attributes (e.g. `data-kernel-audit-row`).
- **Risks**:
  - CSS budget ضغط خفيف (kernel.css 1852→3387). لو وصلنا 5000 سطر، نفصل sections 22–25 في `kernel-interactive.css`.
  - Drawer focus trap غير مكتمل (يحتاج future work).
  - Generate button قد يكدس DOM nodes بدون حد.

### DEC-FIREWALL-001 — AI Firewall Engine Location (Next.js Routes + Prisma)

- **Date**: 2026-07-08
- **Context**: الـ user طلب بناء AI Firewall Engine متعدد الطبقات (5 layers). البريف يفترض Fastify + Redis Streams + BullMQ + ClickHouse + Express backend. لكن البريف ما يطابق الـ stack الفعلي: (1) `frontend/` directory محذوف في 2026-06-29 (DEC-011)، (2) `brightai-api.onrender.com` host ما عاد موجود، (3) الـ stack الفعلي = Astro 6.4.6 static + Next.js 15 + Clerk + Prisma + Postgres 16 + Redis 7 (في docker-compose).
- **Decision**: نبني الـ engine داخل `apps/dashboard/lib/firewall/` كـ Next.js Routes (`apps/dashboard/app/api/firewall/*`) مع Prisma ORM. ما ننشئ `apps/firewall/` workspace جديد. ما نستخدم Fastify. السبب: نعيد استخدام Clerk middleware + Prisma schema + Vitest setup + env validation = 0 infra delta.
- **Rationale**:
  - **`apps/dashboard/` كل شي جاهز** — Clerk auth, Prisma with RLS, withTenant wrapper, env validation (`@brightai/config`), React Query.
  - **Render service واحد** — لا deploy target جديد، لا Dockerfile جديد، لا Render plan جديد.
  - **Prisma models جاهزة** — `AIRequest` (مع `piiDetected`, `piiTypes`, `riskScore`, `riskLevel`)، `AuditLog` (مع `prevHash`/`hash` chain)، `Policy` (مع `rules Json`)، `Organization.compliance: ComplianceFramework[]` — كل الحقول موجودة من Phase 0.
  - **التوسع ممكن** — لو Phase 2 (Risk Scoring ML) احتاج GPU workers، نـ split لـ `apps/firewall-workers/` (BullMQ) في وقتها. Phase 1a/1b/1c ما تحتاج هذا.
- **Reversal cost**: عالية. فصل الـ engine لـ workspace مستقل يحتاج: (1) نقل 17 ملف، (2) إضافة Dockerfile + render.yaml entry، (3) ضبط CORS، (4) تحديث CSP في `render.yaml`، (5) إعادة publish كل الـ tests.
- **Do not create `apps/firewall/` workspace** بدون موافقة صريحة + خطة فصل كاملة.
- **Risks**:
  - Render `starter` plan (512MB RAM) — Phase 1a (regex-only) يشتغل. Phase 1b (NER XLM-RoBERTa ~278MB) يحتاج upgrade لـ `standard` (2GB).
  - Tenant resolution: الـ routes تستخدم API key auth (orgId يأتي من ApiKey.organizationId). Clerk session auth يحتاج tenant resolution من `x-tenant-slug` header — Phase 1b يضيفها.

### DEC-FIREWALL-002 — Detector Strategy (Regex-first, NER Phase 1b)

- **Date**: 2026-07-08
- **Context**: المرحلة الأولى (Layer 1) تحتاج PII Detection للـ PII types سعودية. الخيارات: (1) Presidio (Microsoft، English-first، بطيء)، (2) `compromise` (English NER، lightweight)، (3) regex بحت، (4) Arabic NER model (CAMeL/AraBERT/XLM-RoBERTa).
- **Decision**: نبدأ بـ **regex-first** (Phase 1a، 14 نمط سعودي)، ونضيف **XLM-RoBERTa Arabic NER** (Phase 1b عبر `@huggingface/transformers` على ONNX runtime) للـ Arabic person names فقط.
- **Rationale**:
  - **Regex = 1-2ms** على 1KB prompt. NER = 50-150ms cold، 20-50ms warm. للـ PII types الهيكلية (IBAN، ID، Phone، Email، Plate، Address، CR، MRN، Credential، Date) الـ regex sufficient ودقيق.
  - **Arabic names وحدها** تحتاج NER — الـ regex ما يقدر يميز "محمد العتيبي" (اسم) من "محمد بن سعود" (ممكن يكون institution) بدون semantic understanding.
  - **XLM-RoBERTa HRL** (278MB) أصغر من AraBERT/CAMeL (543MB each) — مناسب لـ Render free tier. PERSON F1 على Arabic 85.4 (4 نقاط تحت الأفضل).
  - **كل النماذج alternatives متاحة كـ Phase 1c upgrade** — لو A/B test أظهر PERSON F1 أقل من المتوقع، نبدل لـ AraBERT.
- **Reversal cost**: منخفضة. إضافة NER models إضافية = swap import في `lib/firewall/detectors/ner.ts`. Regex patterns = إضافة/تعديل في `regex.ts`.
- **Do not** تستخدم Presidio (ثقيل، بطيء، ما يفهم عربي). **Do not** تنشئ regex مخصص لكل dialect عربي (overengineering).
- **Risks**:
  - **False positives** على 10-digit numbers (مو كلهم IDs) — نخفف بـ lookbehind/lookahead `(?<!\d)...(?!\d)`. في 14 pattern موجودة.
  - **NER cold-start ~2s** على أول request — نخفف بـ lazy load + pre-warm في `instrumentation.ts` (Phase 1b).
  - **Confidence calibration** — الـ corpus regression test (Phase 1b يضيف 1000 sample) يحسب recall/FP rate per type.

### DEC-FIREWALL-003 — Default Action = Mask (Configurable Per Request)

- **Date**: 2026-07-08
- **Context**: الـ 4 actions (redact | mask | tokenize | block) لها tradeoffs مختلفة. السؤال: ما الـ default؟
- **Decision**: **Default = `mask`** لكل PII types. الـ caller يقدر يـ override عبر `policy.actions[PIIType]` (per-type) أو `body.action` (global override في `/redact` endpoint).
- **Rationale**:
  - **Mask = balance** بين privacy (الـ LLM ما يشوف القيمة الكاملة) و utility (الـ LLM يفهم السياق). مثلاً `1********3` يعطي الـ LLM فكرة "هذا رقم هاتف سعودي" بدون كشف كامل.
  - **Redact = aggressive** — `[REDACTED:SAUDI_ID]` يخسر كل السياق. مفيد في log streaming، مضر في chat.
  - **Tokenize = reversible** — يتطلب vault infrastructure (Phase 1c). مو Phase 1a MVP.
  - **Block = fail-closed** — يرجع 451. مخصص لـ CRITICAL (CREDENTIAL leak) فقط.
- **Reversal cost**: منخفضة. تغيير الـ default = تعديل `DEFAULT_POLICY.actions` في `lib/firewall/detectors/types.ts`.
- **Do not** تستخدم `redact` كـ default — يضر UX. **Do not** تستخدم `block` كـ default — fail-closed everywhere = منتج غير قابل للاستخدام.
- **Risks**:
  - **Mask تسرّب partial info** — `1***3` يكشف first/last char. للـ PII types عالي الحساسية (SAUDI_ID، CREDENTIAL)، الـ tenant يقدر يـ override لـ `redact` عبر policy UI (Phase 1c).
  - **`blockOnCritical` flag** — لو true (default)، أي detection مع action=block short-circuits بـ HTTP 451. الـ tenant يقدر يعطله عبر `blockOnCritical: false` (للـ testing).

### DEC-FIREWALL-004 — Audit Chain Algorithm (SHA-256 on Org Level)

- **Date**: 2026-07-08
- **Context**: PDPL Art. 19 (record of processing activities) و NCA ECC 2-2024 §5 يتطلبان immutable audit trail. الـ Prisma schema جاهز بـ `AuditLog.prevHash`/`hash` لكن ما في implementation. السؤال: أي hashing algorithm + concurrency strategy؟
- **Decision**: **SHA-256 chain** على مستوى الـ org (org-scoped chain). كل row hash = `SHA-256(prevHash || canonicalJson(row without hash))`. Concurrency: **Postgres advisory lock** (`pg_advisory_xact_lock`) على الـ org id.
- **Rationale**:
  - **SHA-256 = industry standard** للـ tamper-evidence. أقوى من SHA-1 (deprecated). أنسب من PBKDF2 (heavy) للـ high-throughput audit (1k req/s).
  - **Org-scoped chains** (مو global) — كل org عنده chain مستقل. Cross-org writes متوازية. لو org A و org B يكتبون في نفس الوقت، ما في contention.
  - **Advisory lock** (مو SELECT FOR UPDATE على row) — `pg_advisory_xact_lock(orgId_hash)` يعزل الـ transactions للـ same org. Lock يتحرر تلقائياً على commit/rollback.
  - **Canonical JSON** (RFC 8785 subset) — keys مرتبة أبجدياً recursively. بدون هذا، `{"a":1,"b":2}` و `{"b":2,"a":1}` يطلعون hashes مختلفة.
- **Reversal cost**: عالية. تغيير algorithm = كل الـ existing chains (إن وجدت) ما تتحقق بعد الآن.
- **Do not** تستخدم global chain — يخلق hot spot. **Do not** تستخدم row-level SELECT FOR UPDATE — locking overhead أعلى.
- **Risks**:
  - **Hash drift on Postgres extension upgrade** — لو `pgcrypto` أو `citext` يتغير، الـ stored hashes ما تتأثر (SHA-256 = pure math). لكن لو غيّرنا الـ canonicalJson algorithm (e.g. sort order)، الـ chain ينكسر. **Mitigation**: version الـ algorithm في الـ row metadata (`metadata.algorithm = "sha256-rfc8785-v1"`).
  - **Performance** — كل write يقرأ last hash + يحسب new hash. ~2-3ms overhead per write. مقبول لـ 1k req/s.

### DEC-FIREWALL-005 — Rate Limit (60 req/min/org, Redis with In-Memory Fallback)

- **Date**: 2026-07-08
- **Context**: الحمايتان من الـ DoS على LLM provider: (1) Rate limit per org، (2) Rate limit per API key. الـ stack عنده Redis 7 (docker-compose). السؤال: sliding window أم fixed window؟ Redis أم in-memory؟
- **Decision**: **Sliding window** عبر Redis Lua script (atomic). **60 req/min/org** للـ chat، **120 req/min/api-key** للـ redact. **In-memory fallback** (Map per-process) لو Redis ما كان متاح.
- **Rationale**:
  - **Sliding window > fixed window** — fixed window يـ allow burst في اللحظة الأخيرة من النافذة (60 req في last 1s من 60s window = 120 req في 2s). Sliding window evenly distributes.
  - **Redis Lua = atomic** — `EVAL` script يـ run في server-side transaction. لا race condition.
  - **In-memory fallback** — لو `REDIS_URL` مو موجود أو Redis down، نعطي degraded mode (`X-Firewall-RateLimit: degraded` header). الـ per-process map يحمي ضد DoS local-scale لكن مو cross-replica.
  - **60 vs 120** — chat أغلى (Groq tokens) من redact (local). 60 req/min/org = 1 req/sec average، كافي لـ enterprise use.
- **Reversal cost**: منخفضة. تغيير الـ limits = تعديل constants في `lib/firewall/ratelimit/redis-bucket.ts`.
- **Do not** تستخدم third-party rate limiter (`@upstash/ratelimit`, `rate-limiter-flexible`) — overkill. الـ Lua script 30 سطر.
- **Risks**:
  - **Redis down** → في-memory Map (per-process). لو multi-replica، كل replica عنده count مستقل = ضعف الـ effective limit. **Mitigation**: alert لو Redis error rate > 1%.
  - **Clock skew** على Redis nodes — Lua script يستخدم Redis server time (`TIME` command). Skew بين client/server غير مهم.

### DEC-2026-040 — Content Pages Canonical Pattern (REPORT-13)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: blog (`/blog/`, `/blog/[slug]/`) + docs (`/docs/`, `/docs/[slug]/`) + hub (`/hub/`, `/hub/[slug]/`) كانت كل صفحة تستخدم inline styles خاصة، markup diverged، بعضها يستخدم raw rgba. وكل صفحة وجّهت بنفس مشاكل pricing page (REPORT-12) لكن بدون unification.
- **Decision**: وحّدنا كل صفحات المحتوى تحت canonical patterns:
  - **Hero**: `.page-hero` لكل صفحة (homepage / pricing / solutions / **blog-index** / **docs-index** / **hub-index**).
  - **Content surface**: `.article-content` + `.article-content--reading` (max-width 46rem, line-height 1.95) لـ prose طويلة (blog article + docs article). Includes typography: paragraphs, h2/h3/h4 with scroll-margin-top, lists, blockquote, `.pull-quote` (gradient border + brand " icon)، code blocks (`.code-block` dark gradient + `.code-copy-btn` on hover) + inline `code` (brand soft tint) + kbd + tables (rounded + zebra rows) + inline images (rounded + shadow).
  - **Article hero**: `.article-hero` (max-width 58rem) + canonical `.chip--brand` category + canonical `.chip` tag hashes (e.g. `#شركة الذكاء الاصطناعي`) + meta row (author / date / reading time) مع SVG icons + arrow-divider فوق الـ tags.
  - **Card listing**: `.listing-card` (320px+ min) + image area gradient (`.listing-card__image[data-tint=...]` 5 tint variants per category) + canonical `.card` hover lift (-4px) + brand glow + 3-line clamp excerpt + meta row (date + reading time) + `.listing-card--featured` variant (spans 2 cols + flex-row image).
  - **Filter pills**: `.blog-filters__btn` بـ canonical chip pattern + counts + active highlight + `.blog-pagination` (prev/next buttons + info).
  - **TOC**: `.docs-toc` (sticky + scroll-spy via IntersectionObserver في `TableOfContents.astro`) + active heading indicator (teal vertical bar عبر `::before`) + smooth scroll on click (respects `prefers-reduced-motion`).
  - **Docs page**: `.docs-page-shell--with-sidebar` (1fr / 16rem grid → 1fr على ≤1023px) + `.article-tldr` (gradient card + brand icon) + `.docs-meta` (updated + edit-on-GitHub inline pill) + `.docs-helpful` yes/no widget + `.docs-prev-next` 2-cell footer.
  - **Docs index**: `.docs-search` pill-style input + `.docs-index__section` (5 sections × 5-10 cards = 35 total) + `.docs-cluster` (pathway panel مع border-inline-start 3px + brand-soft bg) + `.docs-quick-links` + `.docs-recent` (date chip + category + title) + `.docs-faq__item` accordion (canonical adaptation من `.home-faq-item`).
  - **Hub**: `.hub-page__hero` + `.hub-card--clean` (4 tinted variants عبر `[data-hub-tint]` attribute selector: teal / indigo / teal-indigo / amber) + `.hub-subpage__hero` + canonical `.hub-related-card`.
  - **Final CTA**: unified `.content-cta-final` (canonical `.card--cta` + h2 + paragraph + actions) يستخدمه docs index + hub index + hub sub-page + blog index via `.blog-final-cta`/.
  - **Reading progress**: `.reading-progress` thin teal bar تحت الـ header في blog article يستخدم CSS var `--progress`.
- **Rationale**:
  - **Single source of design truth** — كل صفحة محتوى نفس الـ anatomy.
  - **Removes 4 rule violations**: 5 inline styles في blog/index + 2 inline max-widths في Layouts + 1 inline padding في hub/index + 35 lines raw rgba في hub sub-page `<style is:global>`.
  - **Future content pages** reuse these patterns بدون inventing جديدة (مثل knowledge base، tutorials، etc.).
  - **Markdown-friendly**: `.article-content` prose rules apply تلقائياً لأي `<Content />` من Astro content collections — لا حاجة لتعديل الـ markdown files.
- **Reversal cost**: Low. كل الجديد في `src/styles/pages.css` (block واحد قابل للحذف) + canonical components في `src/components/`.
- **Do not add new bespoke content-page styles** بدون ما تستنزف الـ canonical patterns أولاً.

### DEC-2026-039 — Pricing Page Canonical Pattern (REPORT-12)

- **Date**: 2026-06-30
- **Context**: الـ `/pricing/` page تستخدم markup قديم بدون canonical anatomy — بطاقات بدون period text أو divider أو featured variant، FAQ بدون styling مخصص أو chevron rotation أو open-state highlight، hero بدون CTAs موحدة، Final CTA بدون banner treatment. كل الـ canonical patterns موجودة في النظام (`.page-hero` / `.card--pricing` / `.home-faq-item` / `.inner-cta-final`) بس الـ pricing page ما كانت تستخدمها.
- **Decision**: وحّدنا الصفحة تحت canonical patterns مع extensions صغيرة:
  - **Hero**: `.page-hero` (radial teal+indigo wash + noise grain) + `.pricing-hero__inner` (52rem max-width centered container) + breadcrumb centered + H1 clamp(2.25→4rem) extrabold + lead paragraph + 2 CTAs (`.btn--glow` WhatsApp + `.btn--outline` Contact).
  - **Pricing cards**: 4 بطاقات في `.pricing-grid--4col` (responsive: 4 desktop → 2 tablet → 1 mobile). كل بطاقة تستخدم canonical `.card--pricing` anatomy + new sub-elements: `.card__pricing-period` (muted, smaller، تحت السعر) + `.card__pricing-divider` (gradient line، بين السعر والـ features) + `.card__pricing-check` (SVG `mdi-check-circle` بدل `✓` glyph) + `.card__pricing-cta` (full-width، anchored bottom via `margin-top: auto`).
  - **Featured card** (الباقة 3 - وكلاء الذكاء الاصطناعي): `.card--pricing--featured` بـ border linear-gradient(135deg, brand-500 → indigo-400) باستخدام padding-box + border-box mask trick + layered shadow (shadow-lg + brand-tinted ring + brand glow) + translateY(-4px) + `.card__pricing-badge` "الأكثر طلباً" positioned absolutely فوق الـ border.
  - **Hover**: translateY(-6px) + shadow-xl + brighter brand glow على الـ featured card، translateY(-4px) + shadow-lg على الـ others.
  - **FAQ**: canonical `.home-faq-item` (glass card) + `.home-faq-chevron` rotate 180deg on `[open]` + new open-state highlight (linear-gradient brand-tinted 0% → solid 60% + brand-300 summary color) + first FAQ open by default.
  - **Final CTA**: `.inner-cta-final` + `.card--cta` (gradient + radial halo + noise grain) + 2 CTAs (`.btn--glow` + `.btn--outline`) في `.inner-cta-final__actions`.
  - **Section rhythm**: `.pricing-section` (max-width 75rem، padding-block var(--space-12)) يطبق على كل section داخل الصفحة.
  - **Mobile overrides**: pricing-hero padding يصغر على ≤640px، grid ينتقل لـ 1fr.
- **Rationale**:
  - **Reuse over rebuild**: كل الـ visual building blocks موجودة في النظام (page-hero، card--pricing، home-faq-item، inner-cta-final) — لا داعي لإنشاء components جديدة. الـ pricing page كانت الـ outlier.
  - **Featured card via padding-box+border-box mask trick**: يحقق gradient border على dark surface بدون box-shadow bleed. Standard pattern.
  - **`margin-top: auto` على CTA**: يدفع الـ button لأسفل البطاقة بغض النظر عن عدد الـ features — يحقق equal-height cards بدون explicit height.
  - **Open-state highlight via linear-gradient + brand-300 text**: subtle visual cue (بدون border change قوي) يحافظ على الـ glass-card aesthetic + يدل على الـ "current focus".
  - **No new tokens**: extensions على canonical classes، لا حاجة لإضافة tokens جديدة في tokens.css.
  - **All 4 packages + 6 FAQs preserved verbatim** + كل الـ 8 sections الأصلية محفوظة بدون حذف أو إعادة ترتيب (قاعدة 2.1).
- **Reversal cost**: Low. كل الـ classes الجديدة في pages.css block منفصل ("REPORT-11 Pricing block extension") — حذفه يعيد الصفحة للـ state القديم. الـ .astro file rewrite قابل لـ git revert.
- **Do not reverse** without explicit user approval + plan for restoring old markup + verifying canonical patterns still apply.
- **Related decisions**: DEC-2026-032 (typography clamp), DEC-2026-033 (color tokens), DEC-2026-034 (card system — .card--pricing foundation), DEC-2026-035 (buttons — .btn--glow/.btn--outline), DEC-2026-036 (homepage ambient patterns — referenced for hero), DEC-2026-037 (solutions page patterns — .pricing-section rhythm reused).
- **Related reports**: `reports/11-pricing.md` (~400 lines, 13 sections, full breakdown + verification + risks + commit message + files & artifacts).
- **Risks**:
  - Featured card hardcoded للباقة 3 — لو المستخدم يبي غيرها، 1-line change في `featured: true/false` flag.
  - Open-state highlight uses raw rgba(42, 138, 126, 0.06) — لو design system يحتاج strict token-only، ممكن يضاف `--brand-soft-highlight` token في follow-up.
  - Comparison table styling untouched (per brief "اختياري") — styling جاهز في components.css line 2069-2107 لو المستخدم يبي تحديثه.
  - 8 internal links + NAP sections preserved verbatim بدون تعديل بصري (للـ SEO internal linking cluster).

## 11. How to Use This File (for future agents)

### At session start

1. Read this file completely (Section 0 through Section 10).
2. Check Section 1 (Project State Snapshot) for current baseline.
3. Check Section 3 (Known Issues Register) for open work.
4. Check Section 4 (Decisions Log) to understand what not to reverse.
5. Check Section 8 (Pending Work) for prioritized next steps.

### Before any non-trivial change

1. Re-read Section 5 (Token & Component Inventory) to ensure no duplication.
2. Re-read Section 3 to see if the planned change addresses a known issue.
3. Re-read Section 4 to ensure the change doesn't reverse a decision.
4. Check Section 2 (Change Ledger) for similar past changes — learn from them.

### After completing a non-trivial change

1. Append an entry to Section 2 (Change Ledger) at the top, following the format in agent.md Section 0.6.4.
2. Update Section 1 (Project State Snapshot) if any baseline number changed.
3. Update Section 3 (Known Issues Register) if an issue was resolved or introduced.
4. Update Section 5 (Token & Component Inventory) if tokens or components changed.
5. Update Section 8 (Pending Work) if the change resolved or introduced pending work.
6. Update Section 10 (Maintenance Log) with the date + what was updated.

### When making an architectural decision

1. Append to Section 4 (Decisions Log) with: date, context, decision, rationale, reversal cost, "do not reverse" guidance.
2. Reference the decision in related change ledger entries.
3. Update Section 6 (Glossary) if the decision introduces new terminology.

### When discovering a new bug or issue

1. Add to Section 3 (Known Issues Register) with: ID (KI-XXX), description, priority, status (`open`), date, notes, affected files.
2. Add to Section 8 (Pending Work) in the appropriate priority bucket.
